# Package net.thevpc.nuts.elem

<a id="pkg-net-thevpc-nuts-elem"></a>

Structured, typed data elements and their serialization infrastructure.



`NElement` represents primitive, array, object, tuple, stream, operator,
and custom values. Builders create and transform elements; readers, writers,
serializers, deserializers, mappers, formatters, selectors, paths, and visitors
support interchange and navigation. Elements can retain comments, annotations,
metadata, source layout, and diagnostics.

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-elem-NElement"></a>

## NElement

```java
public interface NElement extends NDescribable, NBlankable, NElementSimple
```

Nuts Element types are generic JSON like parsable objects. elements are a superset of JSON actually
that support multiple structured elements including json, xml, etc...
Elements are used to provide a convenient way to manipulate structured elements regardless of the underlying
format. Hence It's used for converting from json to xml as an example among many other use cases in the NAF
(Nuts Application Framework)

**Author:** thevpc

**Since:** 0.5.6

<a id="m-NElement-isEmpty"></a>

#### isEmpty

```java
boolean isEmpty()
```

return true if this element is empty:


- primitives are empty only if they are null or an empty string
- objects are empty if they do not have any field
- arrays are empty if they do not have any item
- customs are NEVER empty

**Returns:**

return true if this element is empty



---

<a id="m-NElement-isBlank"></a>

#### isBlank

```java
@Override
boolean isBlank()
```

return true if this element is blank:


- primitives are blank only if they are null or a blank string
- objects are blank if they do not have any field
- arrays are blank if they do not have any item
- customs are NEVER blank

**Returns:**

return true if this element is blank

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-elem-NElementAnnotation"></a>

## NElementAnnotation

```java
public interface NElementAnnotation extends NAffix
```

Nuts Element types are generic JSON like parsable objects. elements are a superset of JSON actually
that support multiple structured elements including json, xml, etc...
Elements are used to provide a convenient way to manipulate structured elements regardless of the underlying
format. Hence It's used for converting from json to xml as an example among many other use cases in the NAF
(Nuts Application Framework)

**Author:** thevpc

**Since:** 0.5.6

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-elem-NElementFormatterStyle"></a>

## NElementFormatterStyle

```java
public enum NElementFormatterStyle implements NEnum
```

Defines the formatting strategies for [NElement](#cls-NElement) serialization in TSON.



These styles represent a layered hierarchy of rules, moving from raw data
preservation to structural reconstruction.



### Enum Constants

| Constant | Description |
| :--- | :--- |
| `VERBATIM` | Preserves the original layout of the element as much as possible. |
| `STABLE` | Builds upon #VERBATIM by ensuring the output is visually clear for human readers. |
| `SIMPLE` | Produces a clean, standalone representation of the element. |
| `COMPACT` | Produces the most dense valid TSON string. |
| `PRETTY` | Performs a total structural reconstruction for maximum readability. |
| `CUSTOM` | Reserved for user-defined formatting logic. |

### Fields

| Modifier and Type | Field | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `private final String` | `id` | lower-cased identifier for the enum entry |

### Constructors

#### `NElementFormatterStyle()`

```java
NElementFormatterStyle()
```

N element formatter style.

| Modifier and Type | Method | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `static NOptional&lt;NElementFormatterStyle&gt;` | [parse(String)](#m-NElementFormatterStyle-parse) | Parse. |
| `String` | [id()](#m-NElementFormatterStyle-id) | lower cased identifier. |

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-elem-NElementNotFoundException"></a>

## NElementNotFoundException

```java
public class NElementNotFoundException extends NException
```

Generic exception to be thrown when an element is not found.

**Author:** thevpc

**Since:** 0.5.4

### Constructors

#### `NElementNotFoundException(NMsg message)`

```java
public NElementNotFoundException(NMsg message)
```

Constructs a new runtime exception with the specified detail message.
The cause is not initialized, and may subsequently be initialized by a
call to [#initCause](#m-initCause).

**Parameters:**

- `message` - the detail message. The detail message is saved for
               later retrieval by the [#message()](#m-message) method.


#### `NElementNotFoundException(NMsg message, Throwable cause)`

```java
public NElementNotFoundException(NMsg message, Throwable cause)
```

Constructs a new runtime exception with the specified detail message and
cause.


Note that the detail message associated with
`cause` is __not__ automatically incorporated in
this runtime exception's detail message.

**Parameters:**

- `message` - the detail message (which is saved for later retrieval
               by the [#message()](#m-message) method).
- `cause` - the cause (which is saved for later retrieval by the
               [#getCause()](#m-getCause) method).  (A `null` value is
               permitted, and indicates that the cause is nonexistent or
               unknown.)


#### `NElementNotFoundException(NMsg message, Throwable cause, boolean enableSuppression, boolean writableStackTrace)`

```java
public NElementNotFoundException(NMsg message, Throwable cause, boolean enableSuppression, boolean writableStackTrace)
```

Constructs a new runtime exception with the specified detail
message, cause, suppression enabled or disabled, and writable
stack trace enabled or disabled.

**Parameters:**

- `message` - the detail message.
- `cause` - the cause.  (A `null` value is permitted,
                          and indicates that the cause is nonexistent or unknown.)
- `enableSuppression` - whether suppression is enabled
                          or disabled
- `writableStackTrace` - whether the stack trace should
                          be writable



---

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-elem-NFragmentElement"></a>

## NFragmentElement

```java
public interface NFragmentElement extends NElement, Iterable<NElement>
```

Array implementation of Nuts Element type. Nuts Element types are generic
JSON like parsable objects.

**Author:** thevpc

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-elem-NListContainerElement"></a>

## NListContainerElement

```java
public interface NListContainerElement extends NElement, NListOrParametrizedContainerElement
```

Object implementation of Nuts Element type. Nuts Element types are generic
JSON like parsable objects.

**Author:** thevpc

**Since:** 0.5.6

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-elem-NMapBy"></a>

## NMapBy

```java
@Target({ ElementType.PARAMETER, ElementType.CONSTRUCTOR, ElementType.FIELD, ElementType.METHOD, ElementType.TYPE })
@Retention(RetentionPolicy.RUNTIME)
public @interface NMapBy
```

Customizes object-to-element mapping and serialization for fields, parameters, and types.



Allows specifying a custom mapper class or overriding the target property name
used when converting between Java objects and Nuts elements (JSON, TSON, YAML).



**Author:** thevpc

**Since:** 0.8.0


---

## NUnaryOperatorElement

```java
public interface NUnaryOperatorElement extends NOperatorElement
```

Array implementation of Nuts Element type. Nuts Element types are generic
JSON like parsable objects.
